Republicans voted to keep them hidden," and "everyone on the Epstein list must be held accountable no matter who they are. Release the files." (source ↗ https://thehill.com/homenews/house/5408200-pelosi-epstein-files-distraction/) The New York Post reported that her spokesperson did not respond to questions about the reversal. (source ↗ https://nypost.com/2025/07/17/us-news/pelosi-calls-epstein-files-uproar-a-distraction-backtracks-an-hour-later/) Five days later she cosponsored H.Res. 581, calling it "a vital step toward truth and accountability in the face of unspeakable abuse" and saying "it is especially shameful that Speaker Johnson has shut down the House of Representatives for the summer to avoid a vote on this resolution." (source ↗ https://pelosi.house.gov/news/press-releases/pelosi-statement-cosponsorship-epstein-files-transparency-act) NOTUS reported she was the highest-profile Democrat to join at that point. (source ↗ https://www.notus.org/house/nancy-pelosi-discharge-petition-epstein-files) She signed the petition and voted for the bill. Four Google News entries with no text record, by their summaries, that she said Prince Andrew should testify, questioned why Hillary Clinton was being made to testify about her husband's ties to Epstein, was reported to have privately criticized Democrats over the vote to hold the Clintons in contempt, and posted that she was voting to release the files.

<a id="h-lateefah-simon"></a>

### Lateefah Simon (Democrat-CA-12)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 3 (3 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Lateefah%20Simon)

An Oversight Committee member who signed the petition and voted for the bill. She was among the sixteen committee Democrats who wrote to Chairman Comer asking for a hearing centered on survivors, a letter recorded under Yassamin Ansari's name. (source ↗ https://ansari.house.gov/media/press-releases/ansari-leads-oversight-democrats-call-for-congressional-hearing-with-survivors-of-epstein-abuse) On the day of the vote she met with survivors and told MS Now's "The Weeknight": "Not only do we center and believe women, we have to hold to account the folks who have seen them as disposable," and "With my 30 years of working for survivors on the streets, I refuse to be a part of a society that doesn’t honor folks who are literally survivors of violence." (source ↗ https://simon.house.gov/media/press-releases/icymi-congresswoman-simon-votes-full-release-epstein-files-and-calls-justice) A second write-up of the same interview quotes her saying "It's going to take women to lead a new revolution." (source ↗ https://www.crbcnews.com/articles/691d5a35b44c12ccf637cc76) A 2026 Google News entry says she boycotted the State of the Union citing justice for Epstein survivors, but carries no text.

**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 3 (3 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Sam%20T.%20Liccardo)

Want me to check a specific document? Fill out this form," with a link for constituents. (source ↗ https://www.facebook.com/repliccardo/videos/1554302785661149/) A 2026 Google News entry says he called for Attorney General Bondi's resignation over the handling of the files, and a Facebook reel addressed what he said the media was not reporting; neither yields text (summary; source text not retrieved). <a id="h-ro-khanna"></a>

### Ro Khanna (Democrat-CA-17)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 11 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Ro%20Khanna)

The bill's Democratic author, with Massie, and the member who has pressed the Justice Department hardest since it became law. When it passed he said "This fight isn’t about politics," and "It’s about justice for the courageous survivors and taking on the Epstein class who have been shielded for too long." (source ↗ https://khanna.house.gov/media/press-releases/release-rep-khannas-bill-release-epstein-files-passes-house) In December he noted that three federal judges had unsealed grand jury material citing his law, adding, "I hope Attorney General Pam Bondi will put the survivors above politics and the interests of the Epstein class." (source ↗ https://khanna.house.gov/media/press-releases/rep-khannas-statement-third-federal-judge-granting-request-unseal-files-ahead) After the December 19 deadline passed he told NPR the delay mattered less than what was withheld: "We want to see the survivors' statements to the FBI, where they name other rich and powerful men who abused them or who covered up the crimes." (source ↗ https://www.npr.org/2026/01/02/nx-s1-5664083/rep-ro-khanna-d-calif-talks-about-the-dojs-release-of-the-epstein-files) He backed survivor Haley Robson's letter asking Judge Berman to compel compliance, after a judge had denied his and Massie's motion for a special master, calling the department's failure "a betrayal of the survivors and a flagrant violation of the law." (source ↗ https://khanna.house.gov/media/press-releases/release-rep-khannas-statement-epstein-survivor-haley-robsons-letter-urging) When the department released about 3.5 million of more than 6 million pages in January he asked "why the rest are being withheld." (source ↗ https://khanna.house.gov/media/press-releases/representative-ro-khannas-statement-dojs-release-additional-epstein-files) On February 9, 2026 he and Massie spent two hours with the unredacted files; the next day he read six names on the House floor, saying, "if we found six men that they were hiding in two hours, imagine how many men they are covering up for in those 3 million files." (source ↗ https://thehill.com/homenews/house/5731909-khanna-epstein-files-house-floor/) The Guardian then reported that the Justice Department said four of the six were unconnected men from a police photo lineup; Khanna responded that "I wish DoJ had provided that explanation earlier instead of redacting then unredacting their names." (source ↗ https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/feb/13/four-men-unredacted-epstein-files-no-ties-ro-khanna) In March 2026 he asked King Charles to meet survivors during his U.S. visit (source ↗ https://x.com/RepRoKhanna/status/2038714850684068153), and in June, after a report that senior Democrats resented him over the issue, posted "I welcome the hatred of the Epstein class." (source ↗ https://x.com/RoKhanna/status/2065378520759472310) On August 5, 2026 he and Massie introduced the Epstein Files Transparency Act II, which would let state attorneys general, survivors and members of Congress sue the attorney general for noncompliance: "Since then, the DOJ has violated our law, delayed the release of millions of files, botched the redactions, and denied the survivors justice." (source ↗ https://khanna.house.gov/media/press-releases/khanna-massie-leger-fernandez-merkley-lujan-introduce-epstein-files) A PBS NewsHour interview in which he said the files were scrubbed to protect powerful men is a dead link, and one Google News entry carries no text.

<a id="h-zoe-lofgren"></a>

### Zoe Lofgren (Democrat-CA-18)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 6 (9 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Zoe%20Lofgren)

She signed the petition the day after it was filed and said so: "I signed the Epstein discharge petition. The heavily redacted files released last night, 97% of which were already in the public record, won’t suffice. We need full transparency now." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepZoeLofgren/status/1963230804877721660) On the day of the vote she told KSBW, "I'm glad that we're having a vote, but the cover-up really makes you think: what's in these files?" (source ↗ https://www.ksbw.com/article/central-coast-representatives-vote-release-epstein/69476881); her own release on the vote survives only as an aggregator's AI summary and a dead link. In February 2026 she reviewed the unredacted files: "Trump and many powerful people are often mentioned, but there’s also MILLIONS of files the DOJ continues to withhold in violation of the law. This coverup needs to end." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepZoeLofgren/status/2021378479304933556) At the Judiciary hearing that week she asked Bondi whether emails in the files warranted investigation; Bondi said the department "will look and investigate any case involving any victim" (source ↗ https://www.cbsnews.com/video/bondi-tells-lofgren-doj-will-investigate-any-case-involving-any-victim-epstein/), and Lofgren posted afterward that "AG Bondi responded with theatrics and regurgitated White House talking points." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepZoeLofgren/status/2021673924984209676) The next day she wrote to Commerce Secretary Lutnick demanding he resign, citing 223 mentions in the files and contacts after he said he had cut ties: "You have demonstrated that you will lie about Jeffrey Epstein for as long as you think you can get away with it." (source ↗ https://lofgren.house.gov/media/press-releases/ranking-member-lofgren-calls-lutnick-resignation-relationship-epstein) After Bondi was fired in April she noted "There is still a bipartisan subpoena to have her testify in front of Congress, and she must comply." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepZoeLofgren/status/2039870915006136473) The Lutnick letter is dated November 2025 in the database; it was issued February 12, 2026.

(source ↗ https://x.com/RepJimmyGomez/status/2002459910257491989) In 2026 he reviewed the unredacted files and posted a video: "To be clear, so many of the victims were children. Words cannot express the shock and disgust after seeing these files." (source ↗ https://m.youtube.com/watch?v=HOhf2a8FERY) During the State of the Union: "Two hours later, and Trump didn't mention Epstein once. To the survivors in the room, it was a reminder of who he protects and who he forgets." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepJimmyGomez/status/2026509047235510627) Days later, sharing an archived photo the department had removed: "Under oath, Howard Lutnick testified that he went to Epstein's Island ONCE with his wife, kids, and nannies. I see none of that here." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepJimmyGomez/status/2027174014305607760) A Ways and Means hearing clip in which he called the format a "dog and pony show" and a dead Facebook video about temporarily deleted files round out the record.

<a id="h-ted-lieu"></a>

### Ted Lieu (Democrat-CA-36)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 5 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Ted%20Lieu)

The Democratic Caucus vice chair, and the member whose claims about the files drew the most pushback. In July 2025 he told TIME that "Speaker Johnson is definitely facilitating a cover up of epic proportions by shutting down Congress early to avoid voting on the Epstein files." (source ↗ https://lieu.house.gov/media-center/in-the-news/house-panel-defies-trump-vote-subpoena-epstein-files) He signed the petition and, before the vote, told reporters: "The president doesn’t want these files out, and one reason we know that is the president can order these files to be released right now." (source ↗ https://lieu.house.gov/media-center/in-the-news/house-overwhelmingly-votes-release-epstein-files) At the February 12, 2026 Judiciary hearing he screened old footage of Trump with Epstein and accused Bondi of lying under oath when she said there was no evidence Trump had committed a crime; Bondi replied, "don't you ever accuse me of a crime." (source ↗ https://lieu.house.gov/media-center/in-the-news/democrats-probe-dojs-alleged-withholding-epstein-files-trump) The New York Post reported that the FBI document he relied on was an unverified 1995 tip from an anonymous limousine driver who later identified himself and has a long anti-Trump social media history. (source ↗ https://nypost.com/2026/02/13/us-news/lieu-makes-wild-claim-about-trump-based-on-unhinged-source/) At a news conference that month he said the files contain "highly disturbing allegations, allegations of Donald Trump raping children and threatening to kill children," per the Hindustan Times (source ↗ https://www.hindustantimes.com/world-news/us-news/ted-lieus-explosive-new-allegations-against-trump-amid-epstein-files-row-raping-threatening-to-kill-101771440952782.html); a fact-check concluded he framed these as allegations to be investigated, not as findings. (source ↗ https://factually.co/fact-checks/politics/ted-lieu-claims-epstein-files-allege-trump-raping-children-b161ab) He and Dan Goldman then wrote to Deputy Attorney General Blanche asking for a special counsel to examine whether Bondi lied to Congress; after the department posted three FBI interview memos in March he wrote: "AG Bondi lied under oath in hearing... DOJ posted 3 FBI interviews related to sexual assault allegations against trump. That’s evidence." The White House called the underlying claims "completely baseless." (source ↗ https://www.newsweek.com/epstein-files-donald-trump-ted-lieu-11632419) Eight of his thirteen entries are copies or clips of the February hearing and news conference; one House link is dead.

<a id="h-robert-garcia"></a>

### Robert Garcia (Democrat-CA-42)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 10 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Robert%20Garcia)

As the Oversight Committee's ranking Democrat he ran the second track of pressure alongside the bill: the committee's August 5, 2025 subpoena to the Justice Department, which he has cited ever since. In early September, after the committee posted 33,000 pages that were mostly already public, he called the release "a joke," said "I know for a fact that [The] White House [is] calling Republican members right now telling them not to sign the petition," and asked, "What is Donald Trump hiding?" (source ↗ https://robertgarcia.house.gov/media/in-the-news/lgbtq-nation-what-donald-trump-hiding-gay-rep-calls-out-cover-over-epstein-files) He signed the petition and on the day of the vote said: "Donald Trump is panicking and trying to stop our investigation. We need to know what he’s hiding, and what powerful men are responsible for the rape and abuse of children and women." (source ↗ https://oversightdemocrats.house.gov/news/press-releases/ranking-member-robert-garcia-statement-house-passing-epstein-files-transparency) On the December 19 deadline he and Jamie Raskin said the administration was "now violating federal law." (source ↗ https://democrats-judiciary.house.gov/media-center/press-releases/joint-statement-by-ranking-members-jamie-raskin-and-robert-garcia-on-trump-administration-s-decision-to-defy-epstein-files-transparency-act-and-continue-epstein-cover-up) When the department released three million of six million pages in January 2026: "they intend to withhold roughly 50% of the Epstein files, while claiming to have fully complied with the law." (source ↗ https://oversightdemocrats.house.gov/news/press-releases/ranking-member-robert-garcia-statement-as-epstein-white-house-cover-up-continues-doj-releases-only-half-of-epstein-files-after-months-of-defying-oversight-committee-subpoena-and-epstein-files-transparency-act) He told Politico the subpoena has no carve-out for ongoing investigations and that "the DOJ cannot be trusted. It’s corrupt." (source ↗ https://www.politico.com/news/magazine/2026/02/05/robert-garcia-epstein-files-oversight-trump-interview-00764831) On February 24, after reviewing unredacted evidence logs, he said "the DOJ appears to have illegally withheld FBI interviews with this survivor who accused President Trump of heinous crimes" and opened a parallel investigation (source ↗ https://oversightdemocrats.house.gov/news/press-releases/ranking-member-robert-garcia-statement-after-department-of-justice-withheld-epstein-files-includes-allegation-president-donald-trump-sexually-abused-a-minor); the department said the file had been pulled temporarily for redactions and that nothing was deleted. (source ↗ https://www.latimes.com/world-nation/story/2026-02-25/democrat-says-doj-withheld-epstein-files-on-trump-abuse-claim) In March he held a Long Beach roundtable with survivor Annie Farmer, who was also his State of the Union guest (source ↗ https://robertgarcia.house.gov/media/press-releases/congressman-robert-garcia-statement-following-roundtable-discussion-epstein), and in June 2026 posted that "Our Epstein investigation is still ongoing. This week, we're talking to Lesley Groff and Bill Gates." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepRobertGarcia/status/2064470396963840152) Three committee or House links are dead copies of live ones, and two Advocate interviews carry no text.

At Bondi's February 2026 Judiciary hearing, held largely on the files, the Denver Post editorial board described him taking "a different tack": playing a January 6 clip, pressing Bondi on the gutting of the Public Integrity Section and the cryptocurrency unit, and concluding, "I think what is happening at the Department of Justice is a disgrace." (source ↗ https://www.denverpost.com/2026/02/17/crow-indictment-justice-department-epstein) A dead Economic Times link says he played Epstein videos at that hearing (summary; source text not retrieved). The third entry, an April 2026 post about Republicans still talking about Biden, does not mention Epstein.

<a id="h-jeff-hurd"></a>

### Jeff Hurd (Republican-CO-3)
**Position:** Marked the vote only · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 1 (1 entry in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Jeff%20Hurd)

He did not sign the petition and voted for the bill. Before the vote he told the Grand Junction Daily Sentinel the action was "way past due": "They should have been released years ago and I haven’t seen a good reason why they weren’t. My view is that we need maximum transparency while making sure that victims are not re-victimized. The American people need to see who enabled Epstein, who protected him, and who may have been involved in criminal conduct." Asked what Congress would do about heavy redactions, he said he would "take it on a case-by-case, document-by-document basis," and called the scandal one "on both sides of the aisle," citing Stacey Plaskett's texts and Alex Acosta's 2008 deal. (source ↗ https://www.gjsentinel.com/news/western_colorado/way-past-due-rep-hurd-votes-to-release-epstein-files/article_47574277-9fce-48fb-aca8-c09efaf2c05a.html)

<a id="h-lauren-boebert"></a>

### Lauren Boebert (Republican-CO-4)
**Position:** Led the push, then redirected to the Clintons · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 7 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Lauren%20Boebert)

One of four Republicans who signed the petition, and the one the White House worked hardest to peel off. On the morning the petition reached 218, top White House and Justice Department officials met her in the Situation Room to get her to remove her name; ABC News reported the meeting and its purpose and quoted her post afterward, "I want to thank White House officials for meeting with me today. Together, we remain committed to ensuring transparency for the American people." (source ↗ https://abcnews.com/US/top-officials-met-boebert-house-vote-release-epstein/story?id=127458397) The Colorado Sun reported that Press Secretary Karoline Leavitt confirmed the effort and that a Trump call to Boebert was described as "unsuccessful" (source ↗ https://coloradosun.com/2025/11/12/lauren-boebert-white-house-situation-room-meeting/), and the New Republic relayed the New York Times report of a "very early wakeup call" from Trump. (source ↗ https://newrepublic.com/post/203100/donald-trump-lauren-boebert-epstein-files-petition) She kept her name on. From the vote onward her own posts turn toward Democrats. The night of the vote: "The Democrats want to talk about Epstein? Let's talk about Epstein's entanglements in the Democrat party." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepBoebert/status/1990908519600554453) Later she wrote that "Bill and Hillary decided they'd rather talk about Bill's escapades with Epstein than spend time in prison (which is where they were headed)." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepBoebert/status/2018749682508312772) She joined the Oversight deposition of Hillary Clinton: "Game on in Chappaqua! I'm here with my Oversight colleagues to depose Hillary Clinton under oath in the Epstein probe." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepBoebert/status/2027067679119405492) After viewing the unredacted files in February 2026 she posted about "Terrifying language in the Epstein Files I viewed yesterday" including "Emails about torture" and "A restaurant called 'The Cannibal'" (source ↗ https://x.com/RepBoebert/status/2021669694453825873); Newsweek, which also quoted her Newsmax interview saying the recruiters around Epstein were "primarily women," noted that the Justice Department has not indicated the files support claims of cannibalism or ritual abuse and that "The Cannibal" is a real restaurant. (source ↗ https://www.newsweek.com/lauren-boebert-sounds-alarm-on-torture-in-unredacted-epstein-files-11509362) Six of her fifteen entries are dead links or duplicates of the others.

**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 8 (9 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Maxwell%20Frost)

An Oversight Committee member who signed the petition, voted for the bill, and kept pressing the department afterward. When Oversight Democrats released photos from the estate in December 2025, he told CNN, per CBS12, "We're not here to point fingers without evidence, but we are here to say the White House is slowing their roll on this entire investigation." (source ↗ https://cbs12.com/news/local/florida-congressman-suspects-white-house-attempting-to-cover-up-of-epstein-files) He reviewed the unredacted files at the department on February 11, 2026 (source ↗ https://x.com/RepMaxwellFrost/status/2021735606200455625) and told TIME that "I read many documents that completely refute what Donald Trump has said in terms of his relationship with Jeffrey Epstein," including witness statements he said contradict Trump's account of barring Epstein from Mar-a-Lago; he used Reddit users to identify document numbers and called for Bondi's impeachment. (source ↗ https://time.com/7379910/epstein-files-trump-maxwell-frost-mar-a-lago/) After Democrats' Palm Beach field hearing with survivors he told MS NOW that Chairman Comer was "engaged in obstructing this investigation and completely doing everything he can to help the White House cover this up." (source ↗ https://frost.house.gov/news/in-the-news/watch-oversight-democrat-tells-james-comer-to-do-his-damn-job-after-epstein-field-hearing) When Bondi was fired he said "I have been calling for Pam Bondi to be impeached or fired, and I'm glad that has now happened," and that her subpoena "remains valid." (source ↗ https://frost.house.gov/media/press-releases/congressman-maxwell-frost-statement-on-pam-bondi-firing) In June 2026 he said the committee had interviewed Bondi and "Her testimony raised more questions than it answered." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepMaxwellFrost/status/2061811257599029741) Two Google News entries (one on a Blanche subpoena call) carry no text.

<a id="h-anna-paulina-luna"></a>

### Anna Paulina Luna (Republican-FL-13)
**Position:** Led the push, then deflected · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 9 (10 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Anna%20Paulina%20Luna)

She chairs the Oversight task force on declassifying federal secrets, and her pressure on the department began months before the petition existed. In early 2025 she told Fox's Jesse Watters, per a Daily Caller report, "I think that those lists need to be released to the American people. That's really up to the decision of Attorney General Pam Bondi," and "as a member of Congress, we have not really gotten much communication from the Department of Justice." (source ↗ https://www.newstalkflorida.com/featured/disturbing-floridas-anna-paulina-luna-calls-out-dojs-silence-urges-bondi-to-release-full-epstein-files/) She introduced the SHRED Act to raise penalties for destroying federal records, telling Fox News "The DOJ has not been really responsive." (source ↗ https://www.foxnews.com/politics/anna-paulina-luna-escalates-doj-standoff-over-epstein-docs-unveils-shred-act) On May 9, 2025 her task force wrote to Bondi demanding the full files by May 16: "AG Bondi needs to stop stonewalling and communicate with us." (source ↗ https://luna.house.gov/posts/breaking-declassification-task-force-demands-ag-bondi-immediately-release-epstein-files-client-list) Then, in July 2025, as the base turned on the administration, she defended Trump; Newsweek quoted her post, "While others were busy stabbing President Trump in the back, we were working overtime behind the scenes," which she said meant the Oval Office, and "This is 4D chess, not checkers." (source ↗ https://www.newsweek.com/trump-epstein-files-letter-anna-paulina-luna-2100571) She did not sign the petition, then voted for the bill. In February 2026 she posted "I honestly don't give a crap if the Epstein prosecutors were Democrats or Republicans. Our justice system FAILED these victims," and opposed clemency for Maxwell, per MEAWW. (source ↗ https://news.meaww.com/anna-paulina-luna-says-justice-system-failed-epstein-victims-across-parties) She told Ross Douthat in June 2026 that a fake passport found in Epstein's safe convinced her "he definitely had intel connections," and that Trump's "hoax" remarks were understandable. (source ↗ https://www.mediaite.com/politics/anna-paulina-luna-discusses-why-she-thinks-epstein-was-an-intelligence-agent-and-trump-opposed-releasing-the-files/) In July she said on a podcast that "we are never going to have the full files" because of a cyberattack during the investigation; a MEAWW fact-check called the claim misleading. (source ↗ https://news.meaww.com/fact-check-is-anna-paulina-lunas-claim-a-cyberattack-destroyed-epstein-investigation-files-true) Two Google News entries on a Maxwell pardon carry no text, and an MSN item on her vote has none.

<a id="h-w-gregory-steube"></a>

### W. Gregory Steube (Republican-FL-17)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 4 (7 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=W.%20Gregory%20Steube)

He did not sign the petition but broke early with the party line in public. On October 3, 2025, while Trump was attacking the effort, the Sarasota Herald-Tribune reported that he "would vote in favor of releasing the famed Epstein Files when the motion likely comes before the U.S. House of Representatives later this month," confirmed by his spokesman. (source ↗ https://www.heraldtribune.com/story/news/local/sarasota/2025/10/03/steube-epstein-files-trump/86484574007/) He ran a "Flash Poll: Release the Epstein Files?" on his House site (the page now shows only the title) (source ↗ https://steube.house.gov/flash-poll-release-the-epstein-files/) and voted for the bill; the Herald-Tribune noted he "was one of the first Congressional Republicans to back the release." (source ↗ https://www.heraldtribune.com/story/news/local/sarasota/2025/11/19/steubes-early-support-for-epstein-files-release-pays-off-after-vote/87335816007/) In February 2026, in a clip shared by Benny Johnson, he said "I've never seen anything like this in my career. I can't explain other than the fact that he obviously was very infiltrated with very rich and powerful people and I guess they decided to look the other way." (source ↗ https://x.com/bennyjohnson/status/2023472286540382267) A YouTube interview is in the record by title only; two Herald-Tribune links are dead duplicates.

<a id="h-lois-frankel"></a>

### Lois Frankel (Democrat-FL-22)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 8 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Lois%20Frankel)

Her district includes Palm Beach, and her record runs from 2019 to 2026 with one target: the prosecutors who gave Epstein his plea deal. The day Epstein died she posted that his death "does not end the need for justice for his victims or the right of the public to know why a prolific child molester got a slap on the wrist instead of a long prison sentence." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepLoisFrankel/status/1160188795834253313) She joined a letter to Attorney General Barr seeking a wider inspector general inquiry (Google News entry, no text). When Maxwell was arrested in 2020 she said "South Florida prosecutors owe the victims and our community an explanation" (source ↗ https://frankel.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=3141), and when an appeals court ruled against survivor Courtney Wild, whom she named, she called the decision "sadly disappointing." (source ↗ https://frankel.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=3250) She signed the petition, spoke on the floor on November 18 (video only), and said in her release that the girls "were failed at every turn," by "a justice system that minimized their suffering, and, shamefully, by Palm Beach County prosecutors who treated them as if they were the criminals." (source ↗ https://frankel.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=4930) In 2026 she wrote to Chairman Comer and Ranking Member Garcia: "I again respectfully request that the full House Committee on Oversight and Government Reform come to Palm Beach County and subpoena key witnesses to testify under oath," and her office said she had advanced two appropriations provisions, one to strip survivors' identifying information from department releases and one directing an inspector general review of Crime Victims' Rights Act compliance. (source ↗ https://frankel.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=5019) Five Google News entries, including two reporting she said authorities "treated survivors like prostitutes," carry no text.

<a id="h-debbie-wasserman-schultz"></a>

### Debbie Wasserman Schultz (Democrat-FL-25)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 8 (10 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Debbie%20Wasserman%20Schultz)

She has pressed the Justice Department on Epstein since 2018. After his death she led a letter to Attorney General Barr, signed with Frankel, Speier, Deutch, Mucarsel-Powell and Raskin, saying that limiting the inspector general "solely into his death is grossly inadequate, and would ignore the various DOJ officials who have allowed this serial pedophile to elude true justice for more than a decade." (source ↗ https://wassermanschultz.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=2387) With Burgess Owens she introduced the Courtney Wild Reinforcing Crime Victims' Rights Act, named for a survivor who went public, to close the loophole the 2008 non-prosecution agreement exploited. (source ↗ https://wassermanschultz.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=3256) She signed the petition and voted for the bill. In 2026 she posted videos saying "The sweetheart deal that Epstein was given covered for his rich friends too" (source ↗ https://www.facebook.com/100044638800139/videos/rep-wasserman-schultz-release-the-epstein-files/1423260955642894/) and "Trump's DOJ is committing the largest criminal cover-up in history. Not ONE Epstein affiliate has been held accountable." (source ↗ https://www.facebook.com/100044638800139/videos/prosecute-epstein-affiliates/785119781236345/) In March 2026 she introduced the No Escaping Justice Act to sanction foreign nationals implicated in the files: "Trump's Department of Justice, under Attorney General Pam Bondi, is too focused on covering up Trump's relationship with Epstein and refuses to investigate the wealthy, powerful individuals who were clearly complicit in these abuses." (source ↗ https://wassermanschultz.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=3471) She reviewed the unredacted files and discussed them on Forbes Newsroom (source ↗ https://www.yahoo.com/news/articles/saw-unredacted-epstein-files-debbie-183718572.html); the Forbes pages saying she accused the department of covering up "vile" conduct are blocked. (link dead as of Sep 2026 ↗ https://www.forbes.com/sites/forbestv/2026/03/24/debbie-wasserman-schultz-accuses-doj-of-covering-up-vile-illegal-conduct-after-viewing-the-unredacted-epstein-files-again/) A Florida Politics item and a Google News entry carry no text.

<a id="h-earl-l-buddy-carter"></a>

### Earl L. "Buddy" Carter (Republican-GA-1)
**Position:** Deflected · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 3 (4 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Earl%20L.%20%22Buddy%22%20Carter)

Running for Senate against Jon Ossoff, he did not sign the petition and avoided saying how he would vote until Trump reversed course. The Current reported that when the Epstein emails emerged the week before the vote he told Fox News, "The Epstein part is just them trying to deflect away from the real problem," and told the Atlanta Journal-Constitution "I trust President Trump's plan to get quickly past this" and that he would "follow [the president's] lead so we can put this hoax behind us once and for all." (source ↗ https://thecurrentga.org/2025/11/17/buddy-carter-skirts-a-reckoning-on-epstein-controversy/) He then voted for the bill and said in his release: "I've said all along that this is a Democrat distraction, designed to deflect from the failure of the Ossoff-Schumer Shutdown. While these games are beneath Congress, it is the Democrats" who, "not Republicans," have "something to hide, and President Trump is leading with full transparency." (source ↗ https://buddycarter.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=16009) WSAV quoted a local survivor of sexual assault who said she had wanted to see "who had their own mind to make a decision" before the president flipped. (source ↗ https://www.wsav.com/news/local-news/rep-buddy-carter-votes-to-release-epstein-files-local-woman-speaks-on-his-decision/) Two entries are duplicates.

**Position:** Worked against release, then marked the vote · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 4 (6 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Ashley%20Hinson)

Now a Senate candidate, she opposed the petition before she voted for the bill it produced. The Gazette reported in July 2025 that she "opposes an effort to force a vote on requiring the Department of Justice to release files" (source ↗ https://www.thegazette.com/news/politics/iowa-s-ashley-hinson-opposes-effort-to-force-a-vote-on-releasing-epstein-files/article_0dc578ce-2d97-524a-a424-0a63f625e462.html), and the Iowa Capital Dispatch quoted her press call: Massie "is approaching this in the wrong way, and he's doing it in a way that we give the floor to Democrats," and "I do trust President Trump here and (Attorney General) Pam Bondi to do the right thing." (source ↗ https://iowacapitaldispatch.com/2025/07/24/what-iowas-federal-delegation-says-about-efforts-to-compel-epstein-list-release/) She did not sign the petition and voted for the bill. In August 2026 American Journal News, a Democratic-aligned outlet, reported that she had missed several Appropriations Committee votes on Epstein amendments in September 2025 while present for other votes that day, and that donors named in the files had given $90,000 to her Senate campaign; the report is the outlet's and is not independently verified here. (source ↗ https://americanjournalnews.com/hinson-missed-multiple-votes-on-epstein-investigation/) That month three callers pressed her on the files during a telephone town hall, and the Gazette reported she "defended her record." (source ↗ https://www.thegazette.com/news/politics/ashley-hinson-pressed-over-epstein-files-during-iowa-telephone-town-hall/article_a9691a7b-8cb6-41ff-9a7a-2fbfd80b2f6a.html) Two Gazette links are dead duplicates and an MSN item has no text.

On May 13, 2026, reposting a survivor's account of finding her name unredacted, he wrote that the department "has still refused to follow the Epstein Files Transparency Law. Instead of protecting survivors, they left their names in the few files they released" while redacting the names of Epstein's correspondents. (source ↗ https://x.com/RepMikeQuigley/status/2054553674597216351) On August 25, 2026: "Nine months after Congress nearly unanimously passed the Epstein Files Transparency Act, the Department of Justice is still hiding 2.5 MILLION files from the public." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepMikeQuigley/status/2092254473758392339) One entry is an AI-generated summary of his own release, and two Fox 32 entries may be the same appearance.

<a id="h-raja-krishnamoorthi"></a>

### Raja Krishnamoorthi (Democrat-IL-8)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 14 (14 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Raja%20Krishnamoorthi)

An Oversight Committee member who has treated the files as a follow-the-money case. He was on Chicago radio about the files twice in July 2025, first after Speaker Johnson sent the House home early (source ↗ https://wgnradio.com/john-williams/rep-raja-krishnamoorthi-we-need-full-disclosure-of-the-epstein-files/) and again on the prospect of a Maxwell pardon. (source ↗ https://wgnradio.com/lisa-dent/rep-krishnamoorthi-releasing-the-epstein-files-is-the-right-thing-to-do/) On August 5, 2025, he introduced a resolution against any clemency for Ghislaine Maxwell: "no one gets to traffic children and then negotiate their way out of accountability." (source ↗ https://krishnamoorthi.house.gov/media/press-releases/congressman-krishnamoorthi-introduces-resolution-opposing-clemency-jeffrey) When the estate documents arrived in September he told CBS Chicago, "we need to follow the money. We need to know who received this money, and who paid out the money," and said of the birthday letter, "It just strains credibility that it's not his signature." (source ↗ https://www.cbsnews.com/chicago/news/rep-raja-krishnamoorthi-review-financial-documents-epstein-files/) He signed the petition and voted for the bill. In 2026 the pace picked up. On May 4 he wrote to Acting Attorney General Blanche demanding that a purported Epstein suicide note, sealed since 2019, be unsealed: "The Department of Justice has yet to make any arrests other than Jeffrey Epstein and Ghislaine Maxwell, despite the existence of millions of documents that suggest a vast network of sex traffickers, including many high-profile individuals at the center." (source ↗ https://krishnamoorthi.house.gov/media/press-releases/krishnamoorthi-demands-release-purported-epstein-suicide-note-presses-doj) At the May 12 survivors' hearing in West Palm Beach he demanded the Treasury's suspicious activity reports: "If we're serious about following the money, I believe that these SARs reports must be disclosed by the U.S. Treasury Department now. Immediately. Today." (source ↗ https://krishnamoorthi.house.gov/media/press-releases/hearing-epstein-survivors-krishnamoorthi-demands-release-treasury-records) With Senator Warren he pressed Goldman Sachs over reports that its CEO tried to keep Kathryn Ruemmler on as an adviser after her Epstein ties surfaced (source ↗ https://krishnamoorthi.house.gov/media/press-releases/krishnamoorthi-and-warren-press-goldman-sachs-ceo-reported-decision-keep-top), and with Warren and Khanna wrote to Barclays' chairman about former CEO Jes Staley. (source ↗ https://krishnamoorthi.house.gov/media/press-releases/krishnamoorthi-warren-khanna-press-barclays-over-former-ceo-jes-staleys-ties) On August 7, 2026, he joined Raskin and Garcia in telling the Speaker to bring the Maxwell resolution to the floor "without asking the permission of President Trump, Attorney General Todd Blanche or anyone else reporting to the White House." (source ↗ https://krishnamoorthi.house.gov/media/press-releases/krishnamoorthi-raskin-and-garcia-urge-speaker-johnson-hold-house-vote-opposing) Also in the record with no readable text: a letter to Blanche against a Maxwell pardon, a letter with Garcia to the intelligence community, a call to reject Blanche's nomination, and remarks to reporters on Bill Gates's Oversight testimony. Four of his committee links are dead copies of live ones.

<a id="h-james-comer"></a>

### James Comer (Republican-KY-1)
**Position:** Led the push, then deflected · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 11 (11 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=James%20Comer)

As Oversight chairman he ran the committee investigation that House leadership offered as the alternative to the bill, and he did not sign the petition. On August 5, 2025, he subpoenaed the Justice Department for its Epstein records and issued deposition subpoenas to Bill and Hillary Clinton, James Comey, Loretta Lynch, Eric Holder, Merrick Garland, Robert Mueller, William Barr, Jeff Sessions and Alberto Gonzales (source ↗ https://oversight.house.gov/release/chairman-comer-doj-complying-with-epstein-records-subpoena/), and on August 25 subpoenaed the estate. When the first 34,000 pages went up and Democrats said 97 percent were already public, he told CNN, "that's 3% more than Joe Biden released when he was president." (source ↗ https://www.mediaite.com/media/news/comer-concedes-on-cnn-97-percent-of-epstein-files-he-released-were-already-public-thats-3-percent-more/) He told the Rules Committee his was "the most thorough investigation into Epstein and Maxwell to date." (source ↗ https://oversight.house.gov/release/chairman-comer-presses-for-transparency-accountability-on-epsteins-crimes-before-rules-committee/) When the estate's birthday book arrived he said, "It's appalling Democrats on the Oversight Committee are cherry-picking documents and politicizing information received from the Epstein Estate today," and "President Trump is not accused of any wrongdoing." (source ↗ https://oversight.house.gov/release/oversight-committee-releases-records-provided-by-the-epstein-estate-chairman-comer-provides-statement/) Of the Trump letter he told CNN, "The president says he did not sign it. So I take the president [at] his word," and said the committee would not investigate "something that was 22 years ago." (source ↗ https://newrepublic.com/post/200211/james-comer-trump-lewd-epstein-note) He voted for the bill. A week later he told Politico, "I fear the report will be like the Warren Report," and "Nobody will ever believe it," and called Ranking Member Garcia "dishonest" and "awful." (source ↗ https://www.politico.com/news/2025/11/26/james-comer-jeffrey-epstein-investigation-00669127) In 2026 he called seven people to transcribed interviews, among them Bill Gates, Kathryn Ruemmler and Leon Black (source ↗ https://x.com/RepJamesComer/status/2028975391730217425); told Hannity, "Donald Trump is completely exonerated in the whole Epstein saga" (source ↗ https://www.yahoo.com/news/articles/james-comer-claims-trump-completely-035348266.html?fr=sycsrp_catchall); and after Nancy Mace's motion carried the committee, formally subpoenaed Attorney General Bondi on March 17, writing that "The Committee has questions regarding the Department of Justice's handling of the investigation into Jeffrey Epstein and his associates and its compliance with the Epstein Files Transparency Act." (source ↗ https://thehill.com/homenews/house/5787966-comer-subpoenas-pam-bondi-epstein-files/) By July 2026 he told NOTUS he agreed with Vice President Vance "100%" that the release had "been handled poorly." (source ↗ https://www.notus.org/house/comer-says-vance-is-100-right-about-epstein-files-delayed-release) One Google News entry on a Leon Black subpoena has no text.

**Position:** Marked the vote only · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 1 (1 entry in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Brett%20Guthrie)

Anyone who committed these heinous acts against minors must be held accountable by law enforcement." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepGuthrie/status/1990880726263201944) Nothing else.

### Morgan McGarvey (Democrat-KY-3)
**Position:** Marked the vote only · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 2 (3 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Morgan%20McGarvey)

(source ↗ https://www.facebook.com/RepMcGarvey/posts/today-we-finally-voted-to-release-the-epstein-files-one-important-step-toward-ju/862016746785158/) A companion video said, "Donald Trump has no excuse not to release the Epstein files." (source ↗ https://www.facebook.com/RepMcGarvey/videos/we-delivered-a-mandate-to-get-transparency-and-justice-for-the-victims-of-jeffre/2192815621127019/) Two entries are copies with no text.

<a id="h-thomas-massie"></a>

### Thomas Massie (Republican-KY-4)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 8 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Thomas%20Massie)

He wrote the bill and the petition that forced the vote. Introducing them with Ro Khanna, he said, "We all deserve to know what's in the Epstein files, who's implicated, and how deep this corruption goes," and "If your Representative won't sign the discharge petition, ask why." (source ↗ https://massie.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=395739) On ABC in September 2025: "We can't avoid justice just to avoid embarrassment for some very powerful men." (source ↗ https://abcnews.com/Politics/massie-epstein-files-avoid-justice-avoid-embarrassment-powerful/story?id=125336706) The petition hit 218 when Adelita Grijalva signed on being sworn in; the day before the vote he told TIME the effort was "not about incriminating the President" and that "I do think part of the reason he doesn't want the Epstein files released is he's trying to protect friends and donors in his social circle of the last four decades." (source ↗ https://time.com/7334230/epstein-files-vote-thomas-massie/) After the 427 to 1 vote he warned, "The Senate should not add an amendment to EFTA to avoid disclosing those rich and powerful men who have evaded justice for so many years." (source ↗ https://massie.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=395780) He marked the law's December 19 deadline (source ↗ https://x.com/RepThomasMassie/status/2001732069496402418), and on February 9, 2026, after reviewing the unredacted files with Khanna, said the department had redacted at least six men "likely incriminated" by the documents. (source ↗ https://www.axios.com/2026/02/09/epstein-files-unredacted-doj-massie-khanna) NOTUS reported he lost his May 2026 primary in part over his split with the White House on the files. (source ↗ https://www.notus.org/house/comer-says-vance-is-100-right-about-epstein-files-delayed-release) In July 2026 he introduced the Epstein Files Transparency Act II to let state attorneys general and victims sue: "Since the Epstein Files Transparency Act was signed into law on November 19, 2025, the Department of Justice has flagrantly and consistently ignored EFTA's disclosure requirements." (source ↗ https://massie.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=395849) Five entries are dead links (Forbes, the Courier-Journal, a BBC item) or duplicates.

**Position:** Deflected, then redirected to the Clintons · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 2 (2 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Steve%20Scalise)

And guess who objected on the House floor? It was Democrats." He said "they hid the Epstein files for four years," and that the estate records showed "a Democrat member of Congress texting with Jeffrey Epstein during a congressional hearing," a reference to Delegate Plaskett. (source ↗ https://www.majorityleader.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=5729) Democrats had objected to the unanimous-consent request in order to hold a recorded roll call instead.

### Troy A. Carter (Democrat-LA-2)
**Position:** Marked the vote only · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 1 (3 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Troy%20A.%20Carter)

<a id="h-clay-higgins"></a>

### Clay Higgins (Republican-LA-3)
**Position:** Worked against release · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** No · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 6 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Clay%20Higgins)

The only member of either chamber on record against the bill. As chair of the Oversight subcommittee that subpoenaed the Justice Department's Epstein files in July 2025, he was one of the few Republicans who voted against that subpoena, AP reported, while pressing to subpoena Bill Clinton. (source ↗ https://apnews.com/article/clay-higgins-epstein-files-124f8e8104f7d39b0b57d0e8b8305386) In September he endorsed Chairman Comer's investigation as the proper vehicle. (source ↗ https://clayhiggins.house.gov/2025/09/03/higgins-declares-support-for-full-scale-oversight-committee-epstein-investigation/) The week before the vote he told NOTUS the petition's Republican signers were friends but "they're all wrong. They're wrong, wrong, wrong about this petition," and told CNN of Trump and Epstein, "You can't control who takes a picture with you." (source ↗ https://time.com/7334984/clay-higgins-epstein-files-house-vote/) He voted No and explained: "I have been a principled 'NO' on this bill from the beginning. What was wrong with the bill three months ago is still wrong today. It abandons 250 years of criminal justice procedure in America," and "If enacted in its current form, this type of broad reveal of criminal investigative files, released to a rabid media, will absolutely result in innocent people being hurt. Not by my vote." He said he would vote for it if the Senate amended it. (source ↗ https://x.com/RepClayHiggins/status/1990868089056219267) The Senate passed it unchanged hours later. Nine of his entries are coverage of that one statement; a town hall item has no text.

<a id="h-mike-johnson"></a>

### Mike Johnson (Republican-LA-4)
**Position:** Worked against release · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 8 (13 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Mike%20Johnson)

The Speaker kept the bill off the floor until the petition made that impossible, then voted for it. In July 2025 he said on CNBC, "What I believe in is maximum transparency, and so does President Trump," while relaying Trump's denial of the birthday letter as "patently absurd" and backing a non-binding Rules Committee resolution instead. (source ↗ https://www.cnbc.com/2025/07/18/epstein-files-trump-johnson-transparency.html) He sent the House home early that month, a Google News entry with no text records. In September he put the Oversight investigation forward as the answer: it "unlike other proposals in Congress, has the force of law, will protect victims, does not require Senate approval." (source ↗ https://mikejohnson.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=2667) Through the October shutdown he kept the House in recess and did not swear in Adelita Grijalva, the 218th signer; on October 21 he said, "If it hits 218, it comes to the floor," and called the petition "that gambit." (source ↗ https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2025/oct/21/mike-johnson-epstein-files-house-vote) On the day of the vote, after Trump reversed, Axios reported he called the bill "dangerously flawed" and said he expected the Senate to fix it (source ↗ https://www.axios.com/2025/11/18/mike-johnson-epstein-files-discharge-petition-vote); Newsweek quoted him saying Democrats were "forcing a political show vote on the Epstein files." (source ↗ https://www.newsweek.com/jeffrey-epstein-files-vote-clay-higgins-released-11068404) He voted Aye. That night, when the Senate passed it unamended, he said, "I am deeply disappointed in this outcome," and "It needed amendments. I just spoke to the president about that." (source ↗ https://www.independent.co.uk/news/world/americas/us-politics/epstein-files-release-mike-johnson-senate-b2868239.html) In February 2026 he said he intended to view the unredacted files himself and was "convinced" the department was "complying with everything they're required to do" (source ↗ https://thehill.com/homenews/house/5731640-mike-johnson-unredacted-epstein-files/); Politico reported he called the department's tracking of members' searches inappropriate (summary; source text not retrieved). (source ↗ https://www.politico.com/live-updates/2026/02/12/congress/member-backlash-to-doj-tracking-00778751) Five entries are dead Google News or Independent links.

**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 3 (4 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Richard%20E.%20Neal)

<a id="h-james-p-mcgovern"></a>

### James P. McGovern (Democrat-MA-2)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 10 (10 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=James%20P.%20McGovern)

The Rules Committee's ranking Democrat, and the second signature on Massie's petition. When Comer came before Rules on September 2, 2025, to defend a 33,000-page release, McGovern asked, "Why can't the administration just release the files? You could do the investigation, but just release all the files," and pressed him on whether he backed the Massie-Khanna resolution. (source ↗ https://www.mediaite.com/politics/mcgovern-hammers-comer-over-epstein-files-dump-97-of-which-is-already-out-in-public/) He wrote afterward, "This week, I was the second person to sign Rep. Thomas Massie's discharge petition," and accused Trump and Johnson of "making phone calls warning members of their conference not to sign on." (source ↗ https://www.jimmcgovern.com/2025/09/12/update-epstein-files/) Before the vote he told WCVB, "Trump is acting like he's guilty of something so let's just release the files." (source ↗ https://www.wcvb.com/article/mcgovern-trump-epstein-files-otr/69442118) He voted for the bill. At a Rules hearing on February 2, 2026, he said survivors believed "DOJ is deliberately releasing personal information about them, while at the same time protecting the men that abused them," and demanded accountability "up to and including the current president of the United States, who is in these files up to his eyeballs." (source ↗ https://newslinklive.com/2026/02/03/mcgovern-accuses-doj-of-deliberately-exposing-epstein-victims-while-protecting-the-perpetrators/) He gave his State of the Union guest ticket to a survivor (source ↗ https://x.com/RepMcGovern/status/2026428703387898034), and in June 2026 posted on reporting about Situation Room meetings on the files: "Why would they freak out if they have nothing to hide?" (source ↗ https://x.com/RepMcGovern/status/2065091111358534037) Two entries duplicate the Mediaite story; a floor clip carries no text.

<a id="h-katherine-m-clark"></a>

### Katherine M. Clark (Democrat-MA-5)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 9 (10 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Katherine%20M.%20Clark)

The Democratic whip. She accused Trump of a cover-up on WCVB in July 2025 and told WBUR she wanted the midterms fought on cost of living and Epstein, per database summaries; her office's reposts of both now return unrelated text. She signed the petition, pressed Republicans to seat Adelita Grijalva, and on vote day took the floor: "This should be an easy vote," and "Even as House Republicans prepare to vote 'yes' today, Speaker Johnson is calling this a 'show vote.' He's calling it 'dangerous.' He's signalling the U.S. Senate to block it." And: "Republicans continue to use their power to shield rich pedophiles from justice." (source ↗ https://katherineclark.house.gov/2025/11/18/2025-11-whip-clark-on-releasing-the-epstein-files-this-should-be-an-easy-vote/) When the December 19 deadline passed she said on CNN that Democrats were weighing "all options," called the department's conduct a "massive cover-up," and said "The time for excuses has ended," while stopping short of endorsing the Massie-Khanna contempt push. (source ↗ https://thehill.com/homenews/house/5659826-clark-epstein-documents-justice-department/) In May 2026, reposting a survivor's remarks before Bondi's testimony, she wrote, "We demand justice for Epstein's survivors." (source ↗ https://x.com/TeamKClark/status/2060438747414450214) Three Google News and WCVB entries are dead.

<a id="h-seth-moulton"></a>

### Seth Moulton (Democrat-MA-6)
**Position:** Took action, then redirected to the Clintons · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 8 (12 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Seth%20Moulton)

His record splits in two, and the second half is aimed not at the Clintons but at a fellow Democrat, Senator Ed Markey, whom he was challenging in the 2026 primary. He signed the petition and voted for the bill. When the January 30, 2026 release went up he posted a six-page document his office had preserved because "the DOJ has already taken the files down once today." (source ↗ https://sethmoulton.com/news/epstein-files/) In April, when Trump removed Bondi, he told MS NOW, "She was about to be deposed in the Epstein case," and "That's why Trump got rid of her 12 days before that was supposed to happen." (source ↗ https://thehill.com/homenews/house/5814617-seth-moulton-donald-trump-pam-bondi-doj-ouster-epstein/) Then, at an August 3 debate, he asked Markey, "Why would you even take a meeting with a lobbyist on behalf of Jeffrey Epstein?" Markey answered, "At no time did I ever meet with anyone related to Epstein on anything that has to do with Epstein at all." (source ↗ https://www.wcvb.com/article/moulton-defends-claim-tie-markey-epstein-files/73379267) WBUR reported that moderators asked for specifics and he gave none, that his campaign's timeline showed no direct contact between Markey and Epstein, and that Moulton had taken $2,000 from the same lobbyist. (source ↗ https://www.wbur.org/news/2026/08/04/debate-markey-moulton-fact-checks-senate-massachusetts) His campaign published the timeline (source ↗ https://sethmoulton.com/markeys-role-in-the-epstein-files/) and demanded Markey return contributions. (source ↗ https://x.com/sethmoulton/status/2085027397666025730) WCVB reported PolitiFact found the email he relied on does not say Markey knew of Epstein's involvement; Moulton said he had returned his own money from the lobbyist. (source ↗ https://www.wcvb.com/article/moulton-defends-claim-tie-markey-epstein-files/73379267) Eleven of his 23 entries concern the Markey attack, and ten are duplicates or dead links; a 2019 post saying "The investigation into Epstein should continue" appears twice.

<a id="h-ayanna-pressley"></a>

### Ayanna Pressley (Democrat-MA-7)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 10 (10 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Ayanna%20Pressley)

An Oversight member who has spoken throughout as a survivor of sexual abuse. She backed the July 23, 2025 committee motion that forced Comer to subpoena the Justice Department's files: "As a survivor, I think about my own experience every single day. It is a life sentence, and we cannot lose sight of the people harmed." (source ↗ https://www.boston.com/news/local-news/2025/07/24/as-a-survivor-of-sexual-assault-pressley-says-effort-to-subpoena-epstein-files-is-personal/) On August 7 she led committee Democrats' letter demanding a public hearing for survivors: "If we are to hold powerful people to account, our investigation must center the voices they tried to silence." (source ↗ https://pressley.house.gov/2025/08/07/pressley-leads-oversight-dems-call-for-congressional-hearing-with-survivors-of-epstein-abuse/) After the September 2 survivors' roundtable she told GBH the files must come out "unredacted, except for shielding the names of the victims." (source ↗ https://www.wgbh.org/news/local/2025-09-04/ayanna-pressley-demands-unredacted-epstein-files-following-survivors-roundtable) She signed the petition, voted for the bill, and spent that day in a closed listening session and a candlelight vigil with survivors. (source ↗ https://pressley.house.gov/2025/11/18/pressley-applauds-house-vote-to-release-epstein-files-continues-push-for-accountability/) On December 19: "Donald Trump and his so-called Department of Justice are now violating federal law as they continue to obstruct the release of the Epstein files." (source ↗ https://pressley.house.gov/2025/12/19/pressley-condemns-trump-admin-for-defying-epstein-files-transparency-act-continues-push-for-survivors-healing/) At a February 11, 2026 press conference with survivors she said, "Jeffrey Epstein can rot in hell, and Ghislaine Maxwell can follow suit. And all of those co-conspirators and accomplices deserve to be named and shamed." (source ↗ https://pressley.house.gov/2026/02/11/watch-pressley-joins-epstein-survivors-to-demand-transparency-accountability-slams-trump-doj-for-protecting-abusers/) In April she called Bondi "a chief obstructionist of that justice" and said, "That subpoena stands and she will have to come before us." (source ↗ https://www.nbcboston.com/news/politics/ayanna-pressley-interview-epstein-iran-trump/3931701/) A YouTube entry showing her with demonstrators outside the survivors' press conference carries no further text.

<a id="h-stephen-f-lynch"></a>

### Stephen F. Lynch (Democrat-MA-8)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 11 (13 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Stephen%20F.%20Lynch)

As acting ranking member of Oversight, he and Robert Garcia wrote to Bondi and Kash Patel on June 5, 2025, the day Elon Musk posted that Trump was in the files: "We ask that you immediately clarify whether this allegation is true." (source ↗ https://oversightdemocrats.house.gov/news/press-releases/trump-suppressing-epstein-files-task-force-ranking-member-garcia-and-acting) He signed the petition and voted for the bill. In 2026 he became one of the committee's main pursuers of Bondi. After her closed briefing in March he said she "could not explain why her Justice Department has failed to pursue a case against Epstein's co-conspirators" and must "testify under oath." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepStephenLynch/status/2034714333582659624) When Trump removed her he said she remained "subject to our duly issued subpoena" for the April 14 deposition. (source ↗ https://x.com/RepStephenLynch/status/2039842151626846674) When she did not appear: "Congress should immediately hold Ms. Bondi in contempt of Congress." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepStephenLynch/status/2044243253680099830) On April 30 he reported that "former Attorney General Pam Bondi has finally agreed to comply with our subpoena to testify in our Committee's Epstein investigation under oath," with a date of May 29. (source ↗ https://x.com/RepStephenLynch/status/2049655769243496517) He told WCVB the first lady's statement on Epstein was "odd." (source ↗ https://www.wcvb.com/article/congressman-lynch-on-the-record-iran-war-epstein-files/70986770) Two C-SPAN entries and a committee copy are dead; two Google News items have no text.

### William R. Keating (Democrat-MA-9)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 1 (1 entry in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=William%20R.%20Keating)

He signed the petition and voted for the bill. His one entry is a video of House floor remarks on the files the next day, November 19, 2025; the record holds no text of what he said. (source ↗ https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IPArQutU5jQ)

<a id="h-andy-harris"></a>

### Andy Harris (Republican-MD-1)
**Position:** Worked against release, then redirected to the Clintons · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 3 (3 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Andy%20Harris)

No own-voice release; everything comes through reporting. Common Sense for the Eastern Shore reported that in July 2025 he voted with his party on the 211 to 210 procedural vote that kept a Democratic amendment on the files off the floor, that constituents then pressed him for an explanation under nearly every post on his Facebook page, and that he "has not issued a public statement addressing his position beyond his recorded vote." (source ↗ https://www.commonsenseeasternshore.org/congressman-harris-faces-online-criticism-over-epstein-vote-but-local-media-remains-quiet) He did not sign the petition, then voted for the bill. His one quoted statement that day was about Delegate Plaskett: Capital News Service reported he wrote on X, "I think she needs to be censured by the House and removed from the (House Permanent Select Committee on Intelligence) for colluding with a convicted felon during a congressional hearing," and "That does not reflect well on the House of Representatives." (source ↗ https://cnsmaryland.org/2025/11/18/marylands-house-members-unanimous-as-epstein-files-release-passes-house-427-1-senate-follows-suit/) A second local entry carries no article text.

<a id="h-johnny-olszewski-jr"></a>

### Johnny Olszewski, Jr. (Democrat-MD-2)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 4 (7 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Johnny%20Olszewski%2C%20Jr.)

He cosponsored the Massie-Khanna measure on July 22, 2025: "President Trump and his extreme conservatives have doubled and tripled down on Epstein conspiracy theories for years, but now claim there's 'nothing to see.' If that's the case, they should have no problem releasing these files for the American public to evaluate." (source ↗ https://olszewski.house.gov/media/press-releases/olszewski-joins-bipartisan-bill-release-epstein-files) He signed the petition and voted for the bill. On February 12, 2026, he went to the Justice Department to read the unredacted files, telling WBAL, "I went because the Department of Justice isn't doing what the law requires," and describing the security screening, notepad-only rule and reservation system members faced. (source ↗ https://www.wbaltv.com/article/olszewski-slams-doj-process-view-epstein-files/70338839) His statement the next day: "Attorney General Pam Bondi and her boss, President Trump, are in violation of the law that he signed. Some of the documents I saw today remain heavily redacted, and even now raise more questions than they answer." (source ↗ https://olszewski.house.gov/media/press-releases/olszewski-issues-statement-after-reviewing-epstein-files) A December WBAL interview and a constituent-meeting story carry little or no text; two entries duplicate the DOJ visit.

**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 3 (3 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Sarah%20Elfreth)

(source ↗ https://www.facebook.com/RepSarahElfreth/posts/122151795554718944/) On March 11 she read the unredacted files at the Justice Department: "It's clear that Jeffrey Epstein and the powerful men around him never thought these files would see the light of day, but because of the powerful courage and advocacy of survivors, justice will prevail." (source ↗ https://x.com/RepSarahElfreth/status/2031788194669207749) In August 2026, opposing Todd Blanche's confirmation, she wrote that he "Oversaw and implemented the Trump Administration's repulsive coverup of the Epstein Files." <a id="h-kweisi-mfume"></a>

### Kweisi Mfume (Democrat-MD-7)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 2 (4 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Kweisi%20Mfume)

An Oversight member, he sat in the committee's September 2, 2025 meeting with survivors and said afterward: "This is not only about the horrific actions of Epstein and Maxwell, but also about the wider network of individuals who enabled, concealed, and even profited from this exploitation. (source ↗ https://mfume.house.gov/media/press-releases/congressman-kweisi-mfume-statement-on-meeting-with-epstein-and-maxwell-survivors) He signed the petition and voted for the bill: "Tonight, here in the Congress, I was a part of the majority voting in favor of finally releasing all of the files to the public. (source ↗ https://mfume.house.gov/media/press-releases/congressman-kweisi-mfume-statement-on-epstein-files-transparency-act-vote) He told Capital News Service the bill "affects the hearts, the minds, the souls, and the emotions of scores of women." <a id="h-jamie-raskin"></a>

### Jamie Raskin (Democrat-MD-8)
**Position:** Led the push · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 10 (14 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Jamie%20Raskin)

The Judiciary Committee's ranking Democrat, with a record on Epstein that starts in December 2019, when he joined Oversight letters to Attorney General Barr and Florida law enforcement on the plea deal and its concealment from victims. (source ↗ https://raskin.house.gov/2019/12/oversight-committee-investigates-epstein-s-favorable-plea-deal) He signed the petition and led Democrats' floor debate on the bill: "If the president is serious about what he's saying, he has the power to release the Epstein files right now." (source ↗ https://cnsmaryland.org/2025/11/18/marylands-house-members-unanimous-as-epstein-files-release-passes-house-427-1-senate-follows-suit/) When the December 19 deadline passed, he and Robert Garcia said the administration was "now violating federal law as they continue covering up the facts and the evidence." (source ↗ https://democrats-judiciary.house.gov/media-center/press-releases/joint-statement-by-ranking-members-jamie-raskin-and-robert-garcia-on-trump-administration-s-decision-to-defy-epstein-files-transparency-act-and-continue-epstein-cover-up) Of the first heavily redacted batch he asked on CNN, "So how can you block out an entire document? It makes no sense." (source ↗ https://www.huffpost.com/entry/jamie-raskin-epstein-trump-cover-up_n_69484c2ee4b0dd220fa0e1ce) On January 31, 2026, he asked Deputy Attorney General Blanche to let his committee review the unredacted files before Bondi's hearing (source ↗ https://democrats-judiciary.house.gov/media-center/press-releases/ranking-member-raskin-requests-review-of-unredacted-epstein-files-in-doj-s-possession), and on February 9 he did: "there were tons of unnecessary redactions in addition to the failure to redact the names of victims," including an email in which Epstein's lawyers relayed that Trump said Epstein "had never been asked to leave" Mar-a-Lago. (source ↗ https://www.huffpost.com/entry/epstein-file-redactions-justice-department_n_698a3ac1e4b0509b81279545) He told the Guardian he "saw the names of lots of people, who were redacted for mysterious or baffling or inscrutable reasons," Les Wexner among them (source ↗ https://www.theguardian.com/us-news/2026/feb/09/jamie-raskin-doj-cover-up-epstein-files), and told Axios a database search for "Trump," "Donald or Don" returned "more than a million results." (source ↗ https://www.axios.com/2026/02/10/trump-epstein-files-jamie-raskin-unredacted) (link blocked at verification, Sep 3 2026; text preserved from earlier fetch) He brought the brother and sister-in-law of the late Virginia Roberts Giuffre to the State of the Union. (source ↗ https://raskin.house.gov/2026/2/raskin-subramanyam-to-bring-family-of-epstein-survivor-the-late-virginia-roberts-giuffre-as-guests-to-trump-s-state-of-the-union-address) On July 21, 2026, he wrote to Blanche, Secretary Rubio and Jay Clayton on what his staff's review described as Epstein's unregistered work for Saudi Arabia, Kuwait, Russia and Israel: "Jeffrey Epstein never registered as a foreign agent. Yet numerous recently released documents now show he acted aggressively on behalf of multiple foreign governments." (source ↗ https://democrats-judiciary.house.gov/media-center/press-releases/ranking-member-raskin-launches-investigation-into-evidence-epstein-acted-as-unregistered-agent-for-foreign-powers-leveraging-ties-to-trump) Two committee links are dead copies; two Google News items have no text; the Hill, Newsweek and Guardian pieces cover the same February 9 review.

<a id="h-chellie-pingree"></a>

### Chellie Pingree (Democrat-ME-1)
**Position:** Took action · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** signed · **Distinct actions:** 6 (8 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Chellie%20Pingree)

When the Oversight subcommittee voted to subpoena the files in July 2025 she told the Bangor Daily News, "The American people deserve to know the truth: about who's in these files, what they did, what they knew and when and about how some of the world's most powerful people could escape accountability for so long." (source ↗ https://www.bangordailynews.com/2025/07/24/politics/washington/jared-golden-chellie-pingree-back-jeffrey-epstein-files-subpoena-joam40zk0w/) Asked by WMTW why she had not pushed for release under Biden, she said it was not on her radar then: "President Trump was the one who said this should be made available to the public." (source ↗ https://www.wmtw.com/article/maine-chellie-pingree-calls-for-epstein-files-release/65513003) She signed the petition; as the shutdown ended she told WGME the House would have the votes once another Democrat was sworn in. (source ↗ https://wgme.com/news/local/represntative-chellie-pingree-says-shes-ready-to-get-back-to-work-release-jeffrey-epstein-files-president-donald-trump) She voted for the bill; WGME reported her saying Americans deserve to know who is in the files and whether powerful people used their offices to cover up crimes. (source ↗ https://fox23maine.com/news/local/maine-delegation-unanimously-votes-to-release-epstein-files-senator-susan-collins-angus-king-representative-chellie-pingree-jared-golden) She later posted that "The DOJ is trying to hide materials from the Epstein files that implicate Donald Trump." (source ↗ https://www.facebook.com/ChelliePingree/posts/the-doj-is-trying-to-hide-materials-from-the-epstein-files-that-implicate-donald/1450205706481059/) When Bondi was fired in April 2026 she called her "the worst, most incompetent, and most corrupt Attorney General in American history" and noted the pending Oversight deposition: "Getting fired doesn't get her off the hook." (source ↗ https://pingree.house.gov/news/documentsingle.aspx?DocumentID=6732) Two entries duplicate others, one of them a Google News shell with no text.

<a id="h-tom-barrett"></a>

### Tom Barrett (Republican-MI-7)
**Position:** Took action, then redirected to the Clintons · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 5 (6 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Tom%20Barrett)

**Position:** Deflected · **Nov. 18, 2025 vote:** Aye · **Discharge petition:** did not sign · **Distinct actions:** 1 (2 entries in the database) · [all their statements ↗](https://epstein-data.com/efta_statements/statements?name__exact=Trent%20Kelly)

The paper reported that he "declined to come out in favor of releasing the files" and quoted him on the Democrats' push: "You know, I wish they had asked four years ago" and "Personally, I think everybody who's involved in any way should be prosecuted, but I think most of that is just for political purposes and political purposes only, and I don't think they truly care about the victims of those cases." He added, "What is made better, whether they're disclosed or not disclosed?" and said Epstein "is about as accountable as you can get. He's no longer here." (source ↗ https://ourtupelo.com/mississippi-congressional-republicans-decline-to-back-release-of-epstein-files-call-push-a-political-distraction/) He did not sign the petition and voted for the bill two months later.
